# CamStack Capability API Reference

> AUTO-GENERATED by `scripts/gen-api-reference.ts`. DO NOT EDIT BY HAND.
> Re-run: `npm run docs:generate`. CI fails on drift (`npm run docs:check`).
>
> Every capability is codegen-mounted as a tRPC router. **tRPC path convention:** cap `kebab-name` → router `camelName`, method verbatim → `camelName.method` (e.g. `pipeline-orchestrator` → `pipelineOrchestrator.getGlobalStatus`). Device-scoped methods take `deviceId` in the input; cross-node calls may add an optional `nodeId` (routing). Named input/output shapes are defined once in [`api-schemas.md`](./api-schemas.md).
>
> Methods marked `(auto)` are framework-injected: `getStatus` when the cap declares `status`, and the three `getDeviceSettingsContribution` / `getDeviceLiveContribution` / `applyDeviceSettingsPatch` when it declares `exposesDeviceSettings: true`.
